// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
        state.advancing = false;
        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
    }
}

fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if stopping {
        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
    }
    Ok(work)
}

pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = clock.offset();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(now, index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work(shared, true, 0)?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    }
}

/// `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` with `{"args":{..}}`: queue one run of a job as the
/// system on the dev clock and drive chunks until it is done or parked. This is
/// what `snapback4 run <job>` forwards to while dev owns the store.
pub(super) fn run_job(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
    use snapback4_core::{JobState, error::Refusal, ir::ProgramKind, wire};
    let service_route=request.url().starts_with("/jobs/");
    let service=if service_route {
        match super::session_identity::principal_of(&request,shared) {
            Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind=="service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Some(principal),
            _ => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","triggering a deployed system job requires a full service token").guide("jobs"))})),
        }
    } else {
        if !shared.personas {return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","/dev/jobs is a development door; use run <job> --release <dir> with a service token").guide("jobs"))}));}
        None
    };
    let refuse = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| respond(request, status, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal.guide("jobs"))}));
    let payload: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(if body.trim().is_empty() { "{}" } else { body }) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(error) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body is not JSON: {error}; send {{\"args\":{{..}}}}"))),
    };
    if service_route && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
    let program = lock(&shared.bound).programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == name).map(|p| p.program.clone());
    let program = match program {
        Some(program) if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job => program,
        Some(other) => return refuse(request, 404, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{name} is a {}, not a job; queries are POST /q/<op>, mutations POST /m/<op>", other.kind.word()))),
        None => return respond(request, 404, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&super::dev_doors::stale(shared, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("unknown job {name}")).guide("jobs")))})),
    };
    if let Some(raw)=payload.get("args") && let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,raw) {return refuse(request,400,why);}
    let mut args = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    if let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object) {
        for (field, value) in fields {
            let Some(kind) = program.args.get(field) else { return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{name} takes no argument {field}"))); };
            match wire::from_json(kind, value) {

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — a browser proof calls the app's own authenticated client.
import type { App } from "./app.js";
import type { Mutation, Write } from "./contract.js";

export type WitnessReason = "not-ready" | "returned-early" | "event" | "arguments";

const handlerClaim = Symbol("witness explicit handler claim");

type Operations = Record<string, unknown>;
export function registerWebWitness(app: App, api: Operations): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.location?.search !== "string") return;
  const enabled = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("witness") === "web";
  const armed = () => { if (!enabled) throw Object.assign(Error("E_WITNESS_DISABLED: add &witness=web"), { code: "E_WITNESS_DISABLED" }); };
  const operations: unknown[] = [];
  const walk = (value: Operations) => { for (const entry of Object.values(value)) {
    if (entry && typeof entry === "object") {
      if ("kind" in entry && entry.kind === "mutation" && !("ephemeral" in entry && entry.ephemeral === true)) operations.push(entry);
      else if (!("kind" in entry)) walk(entry as Operations);
    }
  } };
  walk(api);
  let ready = false;
  void app.ready.then(result => { ready = result.ok; }, () => {});
  let form: { terminal: Promise<unknown>; cancel: () => void; claimHandler: (owner: App, event: Event) => { mutate: App["mutate"]; done: () => void } | undefined } | undefined;
  Object.defineProperty(window, "__snapback4Witness", { configurable: true, value: {
    ready: () => app.ready,
    viewer: () => app.client?.viewer(),
    clock: () => {
      armed();
      if (!app.client) throw Error("E_WITNESS_AUTH: no signed-in client clock");
      return app.client.now();
    },

/** Run an event handler with an explicit write claim that survives awaited uploads.
 * @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11 — only explicit per-arm custody survives an async handler.
 * Call synchronously from the clicked control. Use the supplied mutate inside
 * the callback; it carries only that arm's token, never another handler's writes.
 */
export async function witnessForm<T>(app: App, event: Event | { nativeEvent: Event }, handler: (mutate: App["mutate"]) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const native = "nativeEvent" in event ? event.nativeEvent : event;
  const bridge = typeof window === "undefined" ? undefined : (window as unknown as { __snapback4Witness?: {
    claimHandler(owner: App, event: Event): { mutate: App["mutate"]; done(): void } | undefined;
  } }).__snapback4Witness;
  const claim = bridge?.claimHandler(app, native);
  try { return await handler(claim?.mutate ?? app.mutate.bind(app)); }
  finally { claim?.done(); }
}

pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = clock.offset();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(now, index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work(shared, true, 0)?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    }
}

        Ok(payload) => payload,
import { DeliveryQueue, memoryDelivery } from "./delivery.ts";
import type { Refusal } from "./contract.ts";
import type { DeliveryRefused } from "./delivery-state.ts";

export function onlineDelivery(env: {
  url: string; fetch: typeof fetch; mint(): string;
  offline?(): void;
  ready(): boolean; current(): boolean;
  headers(): Record<string, string>; revision(): number;
  floor(): number; store(): string | undefined;
  observe(json: Record<string, unknown>, floor: number): void;
  obsolete(why: Refusal | undefined, revision: number): Promise<boolean>;
  refresh(): Promise<unknown> | null;
  onRefused?: ((event: DeliveryRefused) => void) | undefined;
}) {
  return new DeliveryQueue({ local: false, storage: memoryDelivery(), mint: env.mint,
    ready: env.ready, onOffline: () => env.offline?.(), onRefused: event => env.onRefused?.(event),
    send: async (mutation, body, signal) => {
      if (env.refresh()) await env.refresh();
      if (!env.ready() || signal.aborted) return { json: {}, accepted: false, deferred: true };
      const revision = env.revision(), floor = env.floor(), store = env.store();
      const response = await env.fetch(`${env.url}/m/${encodeURIComponent(mutation)}`, {
        method: "POST", headers: env.headers(), body: JSON.stringify({ ...body, store_id: store, retry_write: true }), signal,
      });
      const json = await response.json() as Record<string, unknown>;
      if (signal.aborted || !env.current()) throw new Error("obsolete delivery response");
      const why = (json.why ?? json.denied) as Refusal | undefined;
      // Missing heads are uncertain, never inferred from a receipt's historical seq.
      const validHead = Number.isSafeInteger(json.head) && Number(json.head) >= 0;
      env.observe(validHead ? json : { ...json, seq: undefined, head: undefined }, floor);
      if (!env.current()) return { json: { ...json, denied: { code: typeof json.store_id === "string" && store && json.store_id !== store ? "E_STORE_REPLACED" : "E_STORE_RESTORE" } }, accepted: false };
      if (why?.code === "E_AUTH") return { json, accepted: false, obsoleteAuth: await env.obsolete(why, revision) };
      return { json, accepted: validHead && typeof json.store_id === "string" && response.status < 500 };
    },
  });
}
}

diff --git a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
index c93870a45c..47bf43b64c 100644
--- a/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
+++ b/snapback4/packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md
@@ -1,5 +1,15 @@
 # Snapback 4 behavior changes
 
+## 0.4.32
+
+- Clock advances coalesce arbitrarily many crossed windows; the 10,000-window cap applies only to `--each-boundary`. Skipped-window replies retain exact counts and preview up to 32 replay commands per schedule.
+- Witness mismatches name argument keys; full armed/actual values live in a private mode-0600 proof log. waitForFresh adds substring advice only within explicit scope and names visible headings on timeout.
+- Proof fixtures validate their URL first and identify refused personas or token fingerprints. Missing frontend output names the project's build:web/build script; withBrowser checks playwright-core first.
+- Clock replay commands carry the live dev port. Each-boundary reasons name every window; configured offsets remain exact. waitForClock waits for the page client's advanced clock sample.
+- Stale online clients after dev reset halt with E_DEV_RESET and instructions to reopen; outstanding writes retain unknown outcomes and are never retried automatically.
+- Fixture aliases support zero-based array paths such as $plans.0.id. Test output explains its columns and explicit --as sweep precedence.
+- The testing guide includes a complete production-account day-two proof and a source-generated proof API table. The wire guide opens with runnable literal envelopes, seed/reset forms and credential headers. Packed qualification runs the examples unchanged.
+
 ## 0.4.31
 
 - Existing stores keep their rows, ids, schema and captured `read through` authority. No automatic data repair or audience expansion occurs. Regenerate declarations and rebuild web, native and Exact clients to pick up the runtime and client changes below; review new correctness notes before deploying edited schemas.
{
  "name": "snapback4",

    "snapback4": "bin/snapback4.js"
  },

//! Schedule boundaries: fixed periods (`every 2m`, `every 1d at 08:00`) and
//! UTC calendar months (`every 1 month at day 1 09:00`, `every 1 year`).
//! The scheduler, the dev clock and `doctor` use only these methods.
use super::Schedule;

pub(super) fn is_zero_months(months: &u32) -> bool { *months == 0 }

/// The latest offset a calendar schedule may name: the end of day 28.
pub const CALENDAR_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS: u64 = 28 * 86_400_000;
/// At most once a century.
pub const CALENDAR_MONTHS_LIMIT: u32 = 1_200;

impl Schedule {
    /// The most recent period boundary at or before `now`.
    pub fn boundary(&self, now: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 {
            let n = i64::from(self.months);
            let mut month = crate::calendar::month_of(now).div_euclid(n) * n;
            if self.month_boundary(month) > now { month -= n; }
            return self.month_boundary(month);
        }
        let every = self.every_ms as i64;
        let offset = self.offset_ms as i64;
        (now - offset).div_euclid(every) * every + offset
    }

    /// The boundary one period before `boundary`: the `periodStart` of the
    /// period that ends there.
    pub fn previous(&self, boundary: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return self.month_boundary(crate::calendar::month_of(boundary) - i64::from(self.months)); }
        boundary.saturating_sub(self.every_ms as i64)
    }

    /// The boundary one period after `boundary`.
    pub fn next(&self, boundary: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return self.month_boundary(crate::calendar::month_of(boundary) + i64::from(self.months)); }
        boundary.saturating_add(self.every_ms as i64)
    }

    /// How many boundaries lie in `(from, to]`, for two boundaries of this schedule.
    pub fn periods_between(&self, from: i64, to: i64) -> i64 {
        if self.months > 0 { return (crate::calendar::month_of(to) - crate::calendar::month_of(from)).div_euclid(i64::from(self.months)); }
        (to - from) / (self.every_ms as i64).max(1)
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — exact: the month start plus the offset in i128,
    // never an endpoint substituted at the i53 edges (only ±285,000 years out).
    fn month_boundary(&self, month: i64) -> i64 {
        let days = crate::calendar::days_from_civil(1970 + i128::from(month).div_euclid(12), i128::from(month).rem_euclid(12) + 1, 1);
        let at = days * i128::from(crate::calendar::DAY_MS) + i128::from(self.offset_ms);
        at.clamp(i128::from(i64::MIN), i128::from(i64::MAX)) as i64
    }

    /// `every 1 month at day 1 09:00 UTC`, `every 1d at 03:00 UTC`, `every 2m`.
    pub fn describe(&self) -> String {
        let clock = |ms: u64| format!("{:02}:{:02}", ms / 3_600_000, ms % 3_600_000 / 60_000);
        if self.months > 0 {
            let (n, unit) = if self.months % 12 == 0 { (self.months / 12, "year") } else { (self.months, "month") };
            let day = self.offset_ms / 86_400_000 + 1;
            return format!("every {n} {unit}{} at day {day} {} UTC", if n == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, clock(self.offset_ms % 86_400_000));
        }
        let every = self.every_ms;
        let unit = [(86_400_000, "d"), (3_600_000, "h"), (60_000, "m"), (1_000, "s"), (1, "ms")].into_iter().find(|(n, _)| every % n == 0).unwrap_or((1, "ms"));
        let at = if self.offset_ms > 0 { format!(" at {} UTC", clock(self.offset_ms)) } else { String::new() };
        format!("every {}{}{at}", every / unit.0, unit.1)
    }

    /// The period and offset fit one of the two shapes.
    pub fn valid(&self) -> bool {
        if self.months > 0 {
            return self.every_ms == 0 && self.months <= CALENDAR_MONTHS_LIMIT && self.offset_ms < CALENDAR_OFFSET_LIMIT_MS;
        }
        self.every_ms != 0 && self.every_ms <= i64::MAX as u64 && self.offset_ms < self.every_ms
    }
}

pub(super) fn clock_status(request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
    let result = (|| {
        let (epoch, reader) = shared.reader()?;
        let note = crate::dev_clock_store::ahead(&reader, shared.now());
        lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
        note
    })();
    match result {
        Ok(note) => { let now = shared.now(); respond(request, 200, json!({"storeAhead":note,"now":now,"skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms()})) },
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use super::super::tests::Host;

    #[test]
    fn kit3_interrupted_clock_drain_refuses_with_clock_evidence_and_queued_work() {
        const CHILD: &str = "SNAPBACK4_KIT3_DRAIN_CHILD";
        if std::env::var_os(CHILD).is_none() {
            let result = std::process::Command::new(std::env::current_exe().unwrap())
                .args(["--exact", "serve::maintenance::tests::kit3_interrupted_clock_drain_refuses_with_clock_evidence_and_queued_work", "--nocapture"])
                .env(CHILD, "1").output().unwrap();
            assert!(result.status.success(), "{}{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stdout), String::from_utf8_lossy(&result.stderr));
            return;
        }
        // Signal state is isolated in this one-test child, never the suite.
    /// fired for is committed with the job row, so a restart never fires
    /// twice for one period; a schedule seen for the first time fires from
    /// its next boundary. Returns the names fired.
    pub fn fire_schedules(&mut self, now: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, 0..self.schema.schedules.len(), None)
    }

    /// One atomic schedule boundary, so the host can release its writer between
    /// schedules. A changed/shortened schema is revisited on the next tick.
    pub fn fire_schedule_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), None)
    }

    /// Admit one historical period on the current clock. In particular an
    /// action's deadline begins at admission, not at the historical boundary.
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — period identity and admission time are distinct during explicit catch-up.
    pub fn fire_schedule_boundary_at(&mut self, now: i64, index: usize, boundary: i64) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let schedule = self.schema.schedules.get(index).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("unknown schedule index".into()))?;
        if schedule.boundary(boundary) != boundary || boundary > schedule.boundary(now) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Input("schedule window must end at an elapsed boundary".into()));
        }
        self.fire_schedule_range(now, index..index.saturating_add(1), Some(boundary))
    }

    fn fire_schedule_range(&mut self, now: i64, range: std::ops::Range<usize>, selected: Option<i64>) -> Result<Vec<String>, RuntimeError> {
        let mut fired = Vec::new();
        for schedule in self.schema.schedules.get(range).unwrap_or(&[]).to_vec() {
            let boundary = selected.unwrap_or_else(|| schedule.boundary(now));
            let key = format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape_key = format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name);
            let shape = digest(&schedule);
            let program = self.programs.get(&schedule.target).filter(|p| matches!(p.kind, ProgramKind::Job | ProgramKind::Action))
                .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Schema(format!("schedule {} names missing job or action {}; restore its target before firing it", schedule.name, schedule.target)))?.clone();
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action && !schedule.service.as_deref().is_some_and(crate::identity::is_service) {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled action requires its declared service seat".into()));
            }
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job && schedule.service.is_some() {
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a scheduled job runs as the system, not a service seat".into()));
            }
            let tx = self.store.transaction()?;
            let previous_shape: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&shape_key], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            if previous_shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|previous| previous != &shape) {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            tx.set_meta(&shape_key, &shape)?;
            let last: Option<i64> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [&key], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.and_then(|value| value.parse().ok());
            if last.is_some_and(|last| last >= boundary) {
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            }
            let Some(_) = last else {
                tx.set_meta(&key, &boundary.to_string())?;
                tx.commit()?;
                continue;
            };
            let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
            if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") {
                args.insert("periodStart".to_owned(), Value::Int(schedule.previous(boundary)));
            }
            if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") {
                args.insert("periodEnd".to_owned(), Value::Int(boundary));
            }
            let id = crate::ids::mint(now, &mut self.minter)?;
            if program.kind == ProgramKind::Action {
                let principal = schedule.service.as_deref().expect("checked service seat");
                validate_inputs(&self.schema, &program, &mut args)?;
                super::actions::authorize(&program, principal)?;
                let raw = serde_json::Value::Object(crate::wire::map_to_json(&args));
                let context = Context::new(principal, args).at(now);
                // @ref LLP 3000.009#5.2 — the boundary and service-seat admission are one commit.
                super::actions::insert_request(&tx, &self.schema, &program, &id, &context, &raw, true, now)?;
            } else {
                #[cfg(feature = "native")]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, self.native.as_deref().map(super::native::Module::digest));
                #[cfg(not(feature = "native"))]
                let program_hash = job_admission_hash(&program, None);
                insert_job(tx.conn(), &id, &schedule.target, &args, &program_hash, now)?;
            }
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key, value) VALUES (?1, ?2) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value", params![key, boundary.to_string()])?;
            tx.commit()?;
            fired.push(schedule.name.clone());
        }
        Ok(fired)
    }
}

use super::*;

#[test]
fn a_request_after_engine_poison_still_serves() {
    let engine = Engine::memory(Default::default(), Vec::<snapback4_core::ir::Program>::new()).unwrap();
    let clock = Arc::new(Mutex::new(crate::dev_clock::Clock::new(true, 0).unwrap()));
    let authoritative_now = lock(&clock).now();
    let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
        dev_identity: None,
        release_requests: Arc::new(crate::release_requests::Gate::default()),
        following: Mutex::new(snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Reader::new().unwrap()),
        project: std::env::current_dir().unwrap(), release_root: Mutex::new(None), temporary: true,
        refusals: dev_refusals::Recent::default(),
        latency: Arc::new(latency::Registry::new(&snapback4_lang::compile(&[]))),
        ephemeral: ephemeral::hub(&engine), ephemeral_rate: ephemeral::Rate::default(),
        wake: Mutex::new(ephemeral::Wake::new(&engine)), polls: Condvar::new(),
        adoption: Mutex::new(adoption::Status::new(String::new())),
        readers: Mutex::new(QueryReaders::new(&engine, &Default::default())),
        engine: FairMutex::new(engine), read_cuts: Mutex::new(read_cost::ReadCuts::default()),
        bound: Mutex::new(Bound { receipt_members: Default::default(), refusal_sites: Default::default(), view_types: Default::default(), server: false, schema: Default::default(), programs: Vec::new(), personas: Vec::new(), diagnostics: Vec::new(), notes: Vec::new() }),
        generation: Mutex::new(1),
        worker_revision: AtomicUsize::new(0),
        committed: Condvar::new(),
        seq: Mutex::new(0),
        personas: true,
        hold_effects: false,
        hold_schedules: false,
        time_audience_clock: AtomicI64::new(authoritative_now),
        clock: clock.clone(), maintenance: FairMutex::gate(maintenance::State::default()),
        entropy: Mutex::new(snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()),
        presence: Arc::new(Live::new(clock)),
        long_waits: long_waits::LongWaits::new(&crate::host_limits::Capacity::default()), change_answers: Mutex::default(),
        asset_transfers: AssetTransfers::default(),
        native: Mutex::new(None),
        auth: crate::auth::Admission::default(),
        uploads: AtomicUsize::new(0),
        route_verification: routes::Verification::default(), owner: None,
        release_committed: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        worker_prepare: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        worker_minted: Mutex::new(None), auth_work: Mutex::new(None),
        request_work: Mutex::new(None),
        upload_work: Mutex::new(None),
        effect_wait: Mutex::new(None),
        poll_wait: Mutex::new(None), poll_resumed: Mutex::new(None), poll_notified: Mutex::new(None),
        query_wait: Mutex::new(None), reader_open: Mutex::new(None), changes_refresh: Mutex::new(None),
        mutation_wait: Mutex::new(None),
    });
    shared.install_ephemeral_wake();
    let poisoned = shared.clone();
    assert!(std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let _engine = poisoned.engine.lock().unwrap();
        panic!("request panic while the engine is locked");
    }).join().is_err());
    assert!(shared.engine.is_poisoned());

    crate::host_limits::prepare_tests();
    let server = tiny_http::Server::http(("127.0.0.1", 0)).unwrap();
    let address = server.server_addr().to_ip().unwrap();
    let serving = shared.clone();
    let worker = std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let request = server.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5)).unwrap().expect("request");
        handle(request, &serving);
    });
    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(address).unwrap();
    stream.write_all(b"POST /seed HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n[]").unwrap();
    stream.shutdown(Shutdown::Write).unwrap();
    let mut answer = String::new();
    stream.read_to_string(&mut answer).unwrap();
    worker.join().unwrap();

    assert!(answer.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 "), "{answer}");
    assert!(answer.contains("\"seeded\":0"), "{answer}");
    assert!(!shared.engine.is_poisoned());
}
use super::*;
use std::{fs, path::Path};

const SOURCE: &str = "use personas alice\ntable rows:\n  owner: principal\n  read <- exists grants[parent = .id]\n  online only\ntable grants:\n  parent: rows\n  until: time\n  expire at .until\n  unique byParent: parent\n  read <- deny\n  online only\nquery point(id: rows):\n  return rows[id]\n";
fn exhaust_read_authority(host: &Host) {
    let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
    let bound = lock(&host.shared.bound);
    let until = host.shared.now() + 3_600_000;
    let mut rows = vec![("rows".into(), snapback4_core::wire::row_from_json(
        &bound.schema.tables["rows"], &json!({"id":"hidden","owner":"bob"})).unwrap(),
    )];
    rows.extend((0..8193).map(|n| {
        ("grants".into(), snapback4_core::wire::row_from_json(
        &bound.schema.tables["grants"], &json!({"id":format!("g{n}"),"parent":"hidden","until":until+n})).unwrap(),
        )
    }));
    engine.load(rows).unwrap();
}
fn latest(project: &Path, op: &str) -> Json {
    let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(10);
    loop {
        if let Ok(records) = dev_refusals::recent(&project.join(".snapback4"), None, None)
            && let Some(record) = records["records"].as_array().unwrap().last()
            && record["operation"] == op { return record.clone(); }
        assert!(Instant::now() < end, "no evidence for {op}");
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10));
    }
}
#[test]
fn memory_query_branch_collects_and_a_bare_response_cannot_reuse_a_previous_cause() {
    let dir = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
    let host = Host::with_evidence(true, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), SOURCE, "127.0.0.1", Some(&dir.0));
    exhaust_read_authority(&host);
    let reply = body(&host.request("POST", "/q/point", "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n", r#"{"args":{"id":"hidden"}}"#));
    assert_eq!(reply["denied"]["code"], "E_RULE");
    let first = latest(&dir.0, "point");
    assert_eq!(first["clause"], "authorization allowance (read)");
    assert_eq!(first["site"], "schema.q:4:3");
    // Deliberately bypass every operation and collector. Only the shared JSON
    // response layer sees this injected E_RULE, just like a new transport door.
    crate::host_limits::prepare_tests();
    let server = Server::http("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let address = server.server_addr().to_ip().unwrap();
    let caller = thread::spawn(move || {
        let mut socket = TcpStream::connect(address).unwrap();
        write!(socket, "POST /uninstrumented HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: 0\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
        let mut reply = String::new(); socket.read_to_string(&mut reply).unwrap(); reply
    });
    let request = server.recv().unwrap();
    let _scope = dev_refusals::response::begin(&host.shared, "/uninstrumented");
    respond(request, 200, json!({"denied":{"code":"E_RULE","message":"injected"}}));
    assert_eq!(body(&caller.join().unwrap())["denied"]["code"], "E_RULE");
    let second = latest(&dir.0, "/uninstrumented");
    assert_eq!(second["clause"], "cause not instrumented");
    assert_eq!(second["code"], "E_RULE");
    assert_ne!(second["requestId"], first["requestId"]);
    assert!(second["site"].is_null());
}

#[test]
fn a_checked_out_reader_keeps_its_source_coordinates_across_publication() {
    let dir = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&dir.0.join("reader.db")).unwrap();
    let host = Host::with_evidence(true, store, SOURCE, "127.0.0.1", Some(&dir.0));
    let (_epoch, reader) = host.shared.reader().unwrap();
    let old_generation = reader.generation();
    let next_source = format!("\n\n{SOURCE}\nquery extra():\n  return 1\n");
    let next = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), next_source)]);
    assert!(next.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", next.diagnostics);
    {
        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
        engine.adopt(next.schema.clone(), next.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
        lock(&host.shared.readers).replace(&engine, &next.refusal_sites);
        *lock(&host.shared.bound) = next;
    }
    assert_eq!(reader.refusal_sites[&("rows".into(), "read rule".into())], "schema.q:4:3");
    let (_, current) = host.shared.reader().unwrap();
    assert_ne!(current.generation(), old_generation);
    assert_eq!(current.refusal_sites[&("rows".into(), "read rule".into())], "schema.q:6:3");
    // Source selection does not consult the now newer shared.bound.
    assert_eq!(fs::read_dir(dir.0.join(".snapback4/dev-refusals")).unwrap().count(), 1);
}

#[test]
fn detached_query_uses_its_checkout_map_when_the_advertised_source_changes() {
    let dir = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
    let store = snapback4_core::Store::open(&dir.0.join("reader.db")).unwrap();
    let host = Host::with_evidence(true, store, SOURCE, "127.0.0.1", Some(&dir.0));
    exhaust_read_authority(&host);
    let generation = lock(&host.shared.engine).generation();
    let shared = Arc::downgrade(&host.shared);
    *lock(&host.shared.query_wait) = Some(Arc::new(move || {
        let shared = shared.upgrade().unwrap();
        if lock(&shared.query_wait).take().is_none() { return; }
        // Pause after checkout and simulate the source-map publication of a save.
        // The reader's schema/transaction remains the generation it checked out.
        *lock(&shared.bound) = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("\n\n{SOURCE}"))]);
        *lock(&shared.generation) = generation + 1;
    }));
    let reply = body(&host.request("POST", "/q/point", "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n", r#"{"args":{"id":"hidden"}}"#));
    assert_eq!(reply["denied"]["code"], "E_RULE");
    let record = latest(&dir.0, "point");
    assert_eq!(record["site"], "schema.q:4:3");
    assert_eq!(record["generation"], generation);
    assert_eq!(lock(&host.shared.bound).refusal_sites[&("rows".into(), "read rule".into())], "schema.q:6:3");
}

impl Host {
    pub(super) fn with_evidence(dev: bool, store: snapback4_core::Store, source: &str, bind: &str, evidence: Option<&std::path::Path>) -> Self {
        Self::with_recent(dev, store, source, bind, evidence, None)
    }
    fn with_recent(dev: bool, store: snapback4_core::Store, source: &str, bind: &str, evidence: Option<&std::path::Path>, recent: Option<dev_refusals::Recent>) -> Self {
        Self::with_capacity_recent(dev, store, source, bind, evidence, crate::host_limits::Capacity::default(), recent)
    }

    pub(super) fn with_capacity(dev: bool, store: snapback4_core::Store, source: &str, bind: &str, evidence: Option<&std::path::Path>, capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity) -> Self {
        Self::with_capacity_recent(dev, store, source, bind, evidence, capacity, None)
    }
    fn with_capacity_recent(dev: bool, store: snapback4_core::Store, source: &str, bind: &str, evidence: Option<&std::path::Path>, capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity, recent: Option<dev_refusals::Recent>) -> Self {
        crate::host_limits::prepare_tests();
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new(
            store,
            bound.schema.clone(),
            bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
        )
        .unwrap();
        let clock = Arc::new(Mutex::new(crate::dev_clock::Clock::new(dev, 0).unwrap()));
        let authoritative_now = if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
            lock(&clock).now()
        } else { i64::MIN };
        let presence = Arc::new(Live::new(clock.clone()));
        engine.set_presence(Some(presence.clone()));
        let refusals = recent.unwrap_or_else(|| evidence.map(|project| dev_refusals::Recent::start(project,
            &engine.store().meta("store_id").unwrap().unwrap(), "127.0.0.1:0".parse().unwrap(), false, false).unwrap()).unwrap_or_default());
        let generation = engine.generation();
        let readers = Mutex::new(QueryReaders::new(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites));
        let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
        dev_identity: None,
        release_requests: Arc::new(crate::release_requests::Gate::default()),
        following: Mutex::new(snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Reader::new().unwrap()),
            project: std::env::current_dir().unwrap(), release_root: Mutex::new(None), temporary: true,
            latency: Arc::new(latency::Registry::new(&bound)),
            ephemeral: ephemeral::hub(&engine), ephemeral_rate: ephemeral::Rate::default(),
            wake: Mutex::new(ephemeral::Wake::new(&engine)), polls: Condvar::new(),
            adoption: Mutex::new(adoption::Status::new(String::new())),
            engine: super::engine_lock::FairMutex::new(engine),
            readers, read_cuts: Mutex::new(read_cost::ReadCuts::default()), refusals,
            bound: Mutex::new(bound),
            generation: Mutex::new(generation),
            worker_revision: AtomicUsize::new(0),
            committed: Condvar::new(),
            seq: Mutex::new(0),
            personas: dev,
            hold_effects: false,
            hold_schedules: false,
            time_audience_clock: AtomicI64::new(authoritative_now),
            clock, maintenance: FairMutex::gate(maintenance::State::default()),
            entropy: Mutex::new(snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()),
            presence,
            long_waits: long_waits::LongWaits::new(&capacity), change_answers: Mutex::default(),
            asset_transfers: AssetTransfers::default(),
            native: Mutex::new(None),
            auth: crate::auth::Admission::default(),
            uploads: AtomicUsize::new(0),
            route_verification: routes::Verification::default(), owner: None,
            release_committed: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        worker_prepare: Mutex::new(None),
        #[cfg(test)]
        worker_minted: Mutex::new(None), auth_work: Mutex::new(None),
            request_work: Mutex::new(None), upload_work: Mutex::new(None),
            effect_wait: Mutex::new(None),
            poll_wait: Mutex::new(None), poll_resumed: Mutex::new(None), poll_notified: Mutex::new(None),
            query_wait: Mutex::new(None), reader_open: Mutex::new(None), changes_refresh: Mutex::new(None),
            mutation_wait: Mutex::new(None),
        });
        shared.install_ephemeral_wake();
        let server = Arc::new(
            Server::http_with_limits((bind, 0), tiny_http::ConnectionLimits { max_connections: capacity.connections(), ..Default::default() })
                .unwrap(),
        );
        let address = server.server_addr().to_ip().unwrap();
        let admission = Arc::new(RequestAdmission::new(&capacity));
        let (s, e, a) = (server.clone(), shared.clone(), admission.clone());
        let worker = thread::spawn(move || {
            for request in s.incoming_requests() {
                dispatch(request, e.clone(), a.clone(), address.ip().is_loopback());
            }
        });
        Self {
            server,
            shared,
            admission,
            worker: Some(worker),
            address,
        }
    }

}

#[test]
fn generation_footer_overflow_returns_a_complete_bounded_refusal() {
    let host = Host::new(true);
    let server = Server::http("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let address = server.server_addr().to_ip().unwrap();
    let caller = thread::spawn(move || {
        let mut socket = TcpStream::connect(address).unwrap();
        write!(socket, "GET /footer HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n").unwrap();
        let mut reply = String::new(); socket.read_to_string(&mut reply).unwrap(); reply
    });
    let payload = json!({"data":"x".repeat((16 << 20) - 20)});
    assert!(serde_json::to_vec(&payload).unwrap().len() <= 16 << 20);
    respond_timed(server.recv().unwrap(), &host.shared, 200, payload);
    let reply = caller.join().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(status(&reply), 200);
    let json = body(&reply);
    assert_eq!(json["denied"]["code"], "E_BOUND");
    assert_eq!(json["generation"], 1);
    assert!(reply.len() < 4096, "overflow must replace the entire partial payload");
}


#[test]
fn stopping_admission_flushes_console_entries_that_cannot_expire() {
    struct Output(Arc<Mutex<Vec<u8>>>, mpsc::Sender<()>);
    impl Drop for Output { fn drop(&mut self) { let _ = self.1.send(()); } }
    impl std::io::Write for Output {
        fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> { lock(&self.0).extend_from_slice(bytes); Ok(bytes.len()) }
        fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> { Ok(()) }
    }
    let dir = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
    let bytes = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
    let (ended, complete) = mpsc::channel();
    let now = Instant::now();
    let recent = dev_refusals::Recent::start_with_console(&dir.0, "store", "127.0.0.1:0".parse().unwrap(), false, true,
        move || now, Output(bytes.clone(), ended)).unwrap();
    let host = Host::with_recent(true, snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(),
        "use personas alice\ntable rows:\n  owner: principal\n  insert <- deny\nmutation add():\n  return insert rows {owner: viewer}\n", "127.0.0.1", None, Some(recent));
    for n in 0..8 {
        assert_eq!(body(&host.request("POST", "/m/add", "x-snapback-persona: alice\r\n", &format!(r#"{{"id":"refused-{n}","args":{{}}}}"#)))["why"]["code"], "E_RULE");
    }
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(60);
    loop {
        if let Ok(records) = dev_refusals::recent(&dir.0.join(".snapback4"), None, None)
            && records["records"].as_array().unwrap().len() == 8 { break; }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "evidence never reached the writer");
        thread::yield_now();
    }
    // Frozen time prevents any due-window flush. The shutdown acknowledgement
    // joins the final save/console handoff, even if the last save is observed early.
    assert!(lock(&bytes).is_empty(), "fixture must keep console output pending");
    let sender = host.shared.refusals.clone(); // Held polls retain sender clones too.
    // This accept loop owns its own listener. The independent request fixture
    // remains live while its shared refusal sink is flushed.
    serve_requests(Arc::new(Server::http(("127.0.0.1", 0)).unwrap()), host.shared.clone(), crate::dev_worker::Supervisor::idle_for_test(),
        vec![], || true, crate::host_limits::Capacity::default()).unwrap();
    // The foreground has a bounded shutdown wait. Under CPU contention it can
    // return before the writer releases its lock; sink drop joins actual cleanup.
    complete.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(60)).expect("refusal writer finished");
    let text = String::from_utf8(lock(&bytes).clone()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(text.lines().count(), 1, "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("count 8; window 1s;"), "{text}");
    assert!(dev_refusals::recent(&dir.0.join(".snapback4"), None, None).is_err());
    drop(sender);
}

/** Private implementation; the returned client has the unchanged Client card. */
export async function onlineCustody(raw: Client, url: string, store: SessionStore, report: (error: unknown) => void, autoStart = true,
  captureAuthority?: AdmissionAuthorityContext) {
  const viewer = raw.viewer()!;
  const key = storeKey("writes", url, viewer);
  // @ref LLP 3000.013.000#4 — a device-less browser app captures each click
  // synchronously; once committed, the journal below owns it as before.
  const capture = captureAuthority ? new OnlineCapture(url, viewer, captureAuthority) : undefined;
  // @ref LLP 3000.013.000#4.4 — captured admissions wait behind recovery, from before signed-in.
  let recovery: Promise<void> | undefined, openGate: (() => void) | undefined;
  if (capture) recovery = new Promise<void>(resolve => { openGate = resolve; });
  // Ids durable in the journal that this runtime replays without capturing; settled ids leave.
  const restoredIds = new Set<string>();
  let active = true;
  let storeRefusal: Refusal | undefined;

  // I13: failed receipt persistence cannot turn a known outcome back into

  // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.3 — failed status saves cannot hide this runtime's
  // observed hold. This overlays presentation only; the journal still owns FIFO.
  const unsavedHolds = new Map<string, { reason: string; revision: string | undefined }>();
  const dispatchRevisions = new Map<string, string | undefined>();
  const recoveredIntents = new Set<string>();
  const listeners = new Set<(write: Terminal) => void>();
  const pending = pendingChanges();
  const identification = new IdentityWait(pending.notify);
  const rawIntents = pendingIntentAccess(raw);
  const intentScope = () => active && !storeRefusal && raw.viewer() === viewer ? rawIntents.scope() : undefined;
  const presentation = new OnlineIntentPresentation(intentScope, pending.notify);
  const stopPending = raw.onPendingWrites?.(pending.notify);

      const journal = await read(true);

      const after = JSON.stringify(journal);

      // save must not turn that known outcome into an admission refusal.

    const result = locked(key, () => locked(record.authority.key, async () => {

import type { Write } from "./contract.ts";
import type { SessionStore } from "./auth.ts";
import { StorageWarning } from "./app-storage.ts";
import { mintId } from "./client.ts";
import { WRITE_HISTORY_LIMIT } from "./writes.ts";

export type Intent = { owner: string; id: string; op: string; args: unknown; storeId?: string | null; dispatched?: boolean; holdReason?: string; statusRevision?: string };
export type Terminal = Exclude<Write, { state: "pending" }>;
export type Journal = { owner: string; backend: string; pending: Intent[]; receipts: Terminal[]; retired?: string[]; retirementFull?: boolean; firstStore?: string; changedStore?: boolean;
  /** `id:nonce` of each browser capture that may survive (LLP 3000.013.000 §4.2). */
  captured?: string[] };

export function observeJournalStore(journal: Journal, store: string): void {
  if (journal.firstStore === undefined) journal.firstStore = store;
  else if (journal.firstStore !== store) journal.changedStore = true;
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — persist dispatch ownership before the request can execute.
export function bindIntentStore(journal: Journal, intent: Intent, store: string | undefined): Terminal | undefined {
  if (store) observeJournalStore(journal, store);
  // Admission may have survived a crash before dispatch binding. Even then,
  // an unbound intent cannot migrate past the first observed store.
  const mismatched = intent.storeId
    ? intent.storeId !== store
    : journal.changedStore || (journal.firstStore !== undefined && journal.firstStore !== store);
  if (mismatched) {
    const refused: Terminal = { state: "failed", id: intent.id, why: { code: "E_STORE_REPLACED", family: "store",
      message: "the intent belongs to a different or unprovable store; whether it executed is unknown; do not retry automatically",
      site: "app.mutate", rewrite: "reconcile this write with its original store before submitting new intent", guide: "sync" } };
    journal.pending = journal.pending.filter(entry => entry.id !== intent.id);
    journal.receipts.push(refused);
    keepReceipts(journal, new Set([intent.id]), true);
    return refused;
  }
  intent.storeId = store ?? null;
  intent.dispatched = true;
  delete intent.holdReason;
  // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.3 — even repeated sent status supersedes an unsaved hold in another owner.
  intent.statusRevision = mintId();
}
// Retirement-protected ids have no safe expiry. Keep about 116 KiB at most;
// saturation permanently fences new admissions instead of forgetting an id.
export const RETIRED_ID_LIMIT = 4096;
/** Mirrors online-capture.ts's marker cap. */
const CAPTURED_ID_LIMIT = 1024;
const unknown = (write: Terminal) => write.state === "failed" && ["E_STORE_REPLACED", "E_DEV_RESET", "E_STORE_RESTORE"].includes(write.why.code);

export function keepReceipts(journal: Journal, pinned: Set<string>, retiring: boolean): void {
  const removed = new Set<Terminal>();
  let excess = Math.max(0, journal.receipts.length - WRITE_HISTORY_LIMIT);
  // Compact old unknown outcomes first, then evict old ordinary receipts.
  // A bulk retirement exceeding the body window still keeps every retired id.
  for (const eligible of [
    (write: Terminal) => unknown(write) && !pinned.has(write.id),
    (write: Terminal) => !pinned.has(write.id),
    (_write: Terminal) => true,
  ]) for (const write of journal.receipts) {
    if (!excess) break;
    if (!removed.has(write) && eligible(write)) { removed.add(write); excess--; }
  }
  const retired = new Set(journal.retired);
  // R6: an id the server may have executed is never admitted again by this app
  // while its record might matter. Retirement preserves every displaced id;
  // ordinary successful-write history keeps its existing eviction policy.
  for (const write of removed) if ((retiring || unknown(write)) && !journal.retirementFull) {
    if (retired.has(write.id)) continue;
    if (retired.size === RETIRED_ID_LIMIT) journal.retirementFull = true;
    else retired.add(write.id);
  }
  if (retired.size) journal.retired = [...retired];
  journal.receipts = journal.receipts.filter(write => !removed.has(write));
}

export function retiredReceipt(journal: Journal, id: string): Terminal | undefined {
  if (!journal.retirementFull && !journal.retired?.includes(id)) return undefined;
  return { state: "failed", id, why: { code: "E_WRITE_RECEIPT_EXPIRED", family: "input", guide: "writes", site: "app.mutate",
    message: journal.retirementFull ? "the online journal's retirement id limit is full; new admissions are blocked rather than risking replay of a prior outcome"
      : "this write id was preserved after a store change; its receipt body is no longer retained and it cannot be admitted again",
    rewrite: "reconcile the prior outcomes with the backend before explicitly replacing this account's write journal; do not retry automatically" } };
}
// @ref LLP 3000.013.000#4.2 — an id known only by its transfer marker was admitted
// and settled; its receipt left the journal, so it is never admitted again.
export function capturedReceipt(journal: Journal, id: string): Terminal | undefined {
  if (!journal.captured?.some(mark => mark.startsWith(`${id}:`))) return undefined;
  return { state: "failed", id, why: { code: "E_WRITE_RECEIPT_EXPIRED", family: "input", guide: "writes", site: "app.mutate",
    message: "this browser already admitted this write id; its receipt is no longer retained and it cannot be admitted again",
    rewrite: "reopen to recover that outcome through the server's record; do not retry automatically" } };
}
const record = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> => !!value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
const id = (value: unknown) => typeof value === "string" && /^[0-7][0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{25}$/.test(value);

function valid(value: unknown, owner: string, backend: string): value is Journal {
  if (!record(value) || value.owner !== owner || value.backend !== backend || !Array.isArray(value.pending) || !Array.isArray(value.receipts) || value.receipts.length > WRITE_HISTORY_LIMIT) return false;
  if (value.retired !== undefined && (!Array.isArray(value.retired) || value.retired.length > RETIRED_ID_LIMIT || !value.retired.every(id))) return false;
  if (value.retirementFull !== undefined && typeof value.retirementFull !== "boolean") return false;
  if (value.firstStore !== undefined && (typeof value.firstStore !== "string" || !value.firstStore)) return false;
  if (value.changedStore !== undefined && typeof value.changedStore !== "boolean") return false;
  if (value.captured !== undefined && (!Array.isArray(value.captured) || value.captured.length > CAPTURED_ID_LIMIT || !value.captured.every(mark => typeof mark === "string" && mark.split(":").length === 2 && mark.split(":").every(id)))) return false;
  if (!value.pending.every(item => record(item) && item.owner === owner && id(item.id) && typeof item.op === "string" && item.op.length > 0 && Object.hasOwn(item, "args"))) return false;
  if (!value.pending.every(item => (item.storeId == null || (typeof item.storeId === "string" && item.storeId.length > 0))
    && (item.dispatched === undefined || typeof item.dispatched === "boolean"))) return false;
  if (!value.pending.every(item => item.holdReason === undefined || typeof item.holdReason === "string")) return false;
  if (!value.pending.every(item => item.statusRevision === undefined || id(item.statusRevision))) return false;
  if (new Set(value.pending.map(item => item.id)).size !== value.pending.length) return false;
  return value.receipts.every(item => record(item) && id(item.id) && (item.state === "sent"
    ? Number.isSafeInteger(item.seq) && (item.seq as number) >= 0
    : item.state === "failed" && record(item.why) && typeof item.why.code === "string" && typeof item.why.family === "string" && typeof item.why.message === "string"));
}

/** Called inside the journal lock. Preserve damaged/foreign bytes before
 * replacing the active record. If preservation fails, sign-in still succeeds,
 * but writes remain blocked until storage can preserve those bytes. */
export function journalReader(key: string, owner: string, backend: string, store: SessionStore, report: (error: unknown) => void) {
  const empty = (): Journal => ({ owner, backend, pending: [], receipts: [] });
  return async (forWrite = false): Promise<Journal> => {
    const text = await store.get(key);
    if (text === null) return empty();

        rewrite: `inspect the saved bytes with await sessionStore.get(${JSON.stringify(side)}); submit new intent with app.mutate(op, args)` }));
    } catch (error) {
      report(new StorageWarning({ code: "E_CLIENT", family: "client", site: "app.mutate",
        message: "The damaged online write journal could not be preserved; its original bytes remain and writes are blocked until storage is repaired.",
        rewrite: "repair sessionStore, then reopen createApp({ url, sessionStore })" }));

    }
            const issued = (await response.clone().json() as { session?: Session }).session;
            if (issued?.token) {

      try { return await fetcher(input, { ...init, signal: aborter.signal }); }

    const scopedFetch: typeof fetch = (input, init) => scopedRequest(input, init);
    let raw: Client | undefined, startClient: (() => Promise<void>) | undefined;
    let notifyPublished: ((published: Client) => void) | undefined;

        notifyPublished = published => { stop = notify(published as LocalClient, origin); };

    let custody: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof onlineCustody>> | undefined;

import type { Write } from "./contract.ts";
import { WRITE_HISTORY_LIMIT } from "./writes.ts";
import type { Persistence } from "./web.ts";
import { claimStore } from "./web-ownership.ts";

export type TerminalReceipt = Exclude<Write, { state: "pending" }> & { upload_identity?: { fingerprint: string; op: string; viewer: string; store_id: string } };

export function terminalReceipts(text: string | null | undefined): TerminalReceipt[] {
  if (!text) return [];
  // Only value-free terminal records cross tab boundaries, never outbox data.
  return (JSON.parse(text) as (Write & {upload_identity?:TerminalReceipt["upload_identity"]})[]).flatMap(write => write.state === "sent"
    ? [{ state: "sent", id: write.id, seq: write.seq, replayed: true, ...(write.upload_identity?{upload_identity:write.upload_identity}:{}) } as TerminalReceipt]
    : write.state === "failed" ? [{ state: "failed", id: write.id, why: { code: write.why.code, family: write.why.family, message: `write refused: ${write.why.code}` }, ...(write.upload_identity?{upload_identity:write.upload_identity}:{}) } as TerminalReceipt] : []);
}

export async function receiptHistory(persist: Persistence, name: string): Promise<TerminalReceipt[]> {
  const shared = `${name}:receipts`;
  // Adoption requires enumeration too. Recheck existence on each lookup so a
  // sibling's later adoption is visible, but a read never creates the window.
  const exists = persist.has ? await persist.has(shared) : (await persist.others?.(shared))?.includes(shared);
  if (!exists) return [];
  const text = persist.read ? await persist.read(shared, "m", "client:write-history")
    : (await persist.load(shared)).find(c => c.s === "m" && c.k === "client:write-history")?.v;
  return terminalReceipts(text);
}

const unknownOutcome = (write: TerminalReceipt) => write.state === "failed" && ["E_STORE_REPLACED", "E_DEV_RESET"].includes(write.why.code);

// @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — a caller's lookup applies adoption's correction rule.
// A shared, verified outcome resolves this client's own unknown replacement
// refusal; every other local outcome wins over shared history.
export function lookupReceipts(shared: TerminalReceipt[], local: TerminalReceipt[]): TerminalReceipt[] {
  const merged = new Map(shared.map(write => [write.id, write]));
  for (const write of local) {
    const prior = merged.get(write.id);
    if (prior && unknownOutcome(write) && !unknownOutcome(prior)) continue;
    merged.set(write.id, write);
  }
  return [...merged.values()];
}

export async function shareReceipts(persist: Persistence, name: string, text: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const held = await claimStore(persist, `${name}:receipts`, false);
  if (!held) return false;
  try {
    const kept = await receiptHistory(persist, name);
    const positions = new Map(kept.map((write, index) => [write.id, index]));
    for (const write of terminalReceipts(text)) {
      const index = positions.get(write.id);
      if (index === undefined) { positions.set(write.id, kept.length); kept.push(write); }
      // @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — a verified outcome resolves replacement's unknown
      // execution. The source device already validated the correction before
      // persisting it; adoption must keep it before deleting that source.
      else {
        const prior = kept[index]!;
        if (unknownOutcome(prior)) kept[index] = write;
      }
    }
    // Corrections retain their position; authored outcomes remain immutable.
    await persist.save(`${name}:receipts`, [{ s: "m", k: "client:write-history", v: JSON.stringify(kept.slice(-WRITE_HISTORY_LIMIT)) }]);
    return true;
  } finally { await held.release(); }
}

/** Poll authoritative server truth after a receipt; a pending prediction is not a commit. */
/** @throws deadline naming label and last value; read or predicate errors propagate. */
export async function waitForServer<T>(read: () => Promise<T>, accepts: (value: T) => boolean, options: { timeoutMs?: number; label?: string } = {}): Promise<T> {
  const timeoutMs = deadline(options.timeoutMs, 30_000), end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
  let last: T | undefined;
  while (performance.now() < end) {
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    try {
      const value = await Promise.race([Promise.resolve().then(read), new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
        timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`)), Math.max(1, end - performance.now()));
      })]);
      last = value;
      if (accepts(value)) return value;
    } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.min(25, Math.max(1, end - performance.now()))));
  }
  throw Error(`${options.label ?? "server truth"} timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; last=${JSON.stringify(last)}`);
}
/** Wait until the page's own client has sampled an advanced server clock. Does not advance or replace that clock. */
/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for invalid advanced time; missing/disabled witness, signed-out client, page error or page-clock deadline. */
export async function waitForClock(page: Page, advancedNow: number, options: { timeoutMs?: number } = {}): Promise<number> {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(advancedNow) || advancedNow < 0) throw Error("E_PROOF_USAGE: waitForClock requires the clock advance reply's now");
  return waitForServer(() => page.evaluate(() => {
    const witness = (window as unknown as { __snapback4Witness?: { clock(): number } }).__snapback4Witness;
    if (!witness?.clock) throw Error("E_WITNESS_DISABLED: registerWebWitness and use witness=web before waitForClock");
    return witness.clock();
  }), now => now >= advancedNow, { ...options, label: "page client clock" });
}

export async function fillRN(locator: Locator, text: string): Promise<void> {
  await locator.focus(); await locator.press("ControlOrMeta+A"); await locator.press("Backspace");
  await locator.pressSequentially(text);
}
/** @throws None at construction; ambiguous text is refused when observed. */

/** @throws locator ambiguity, unavailable option or Playwright deadline. */
export async function selectByValue(locator: Locator, value: string): Promise<void> { await locator.selectOption({ value }); }

export async function withBrowser<T>(options: LaunchOptions & Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> & { chromium?: BrowserType }, proof: (context: BrowserContext) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const { chromium: suppliedChromium, executablePath, cwd: _cwd, logsDir: _logs, ...other } = options;

      let path: string;

      catch (error) { if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "MODULE_NOT_FOUND") continue; throw error; }
      const peerModule = await import(path);
      caller = (peerModule.chromium ?? peerModule.default?.chromium) as BrowserType;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!caller) {
    try { caller = (await import("playwright-core")).chromium; }
    catch (cause) { throw new Error("E_PROOF_BROWSER: withBrowser requires playwright-core; run npm install --save-dev playwright-core before deploying or starting proof hosts", { cause }); }
  }
  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const err = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.browser.stderr.log`), "wx", 0o600);
  const diagnostic = openSync(join(directory, `${randomUUID()}.witness.private.jsonl`), "wx", 0o600);

  const launch = { ...other, ...(executablePath === undefined ? {} : { executablePath }), timeout: deadline(options.timeout, 30_000), logger: {
    isEnabled: () => true,
    log: (name: string, severity: string, message: string | Error) => { writeSync(err, `${name} ${severity}: ${String(message)}\n`); },
  } };
  const args = [...(options.args ?? [])];
  if (process.getuid?.() === 0 && !args.includes("--no-sandbox")) args.push("--no-sandbox");
  // Playwright owns its browser child over a pipe, including parent-exit cleanup.
  // No WebSocket server or TCP debugging port is opened.
  let browser;
  try {
    try { browser = await caller.launch({ ...launch, args, chromiumSandbox: !args.includes("--no-sandbox") }); }
    catch (error) {
      if (args.includes("--no-sandbox") || !sandboxUnavailable(error)) throw error;
      browser = await caller.launch({ ...launch, args: [...args, "--no-sandbox"], chromiumSandbox: false });
    }
    let context: BrowserContext | undefined;
    try {
      context = await browser.newContext();
      await context.exposeBinding("__snapback4ProofDiagnostic", (_source, value: unknown) => {
        writeSync(diagnostic, `${JSON.stringify(value)}\n`);
      });
      // Init scripts run on each document, including HTTP redirect destinations,
      // before any application script samples its URL.
      await context.addInitScript(() => {
        if (window.top !== window || !/^https?:$/.test(location.protocol)) return;
        const url = new URL(location.href);
        url.searchParams.set("witness", "web");

      context.setDefaultTimeout(30_000); context.setDefaultNavigationTimeout(30_000);
      return await proof(context);
    } finally {
      try { if (context) await bounded(context.close(), 5000, "browser context close"); }
      finally { await bounded(browser.close(), 5000, "browser close"); }
    }
  } finally { closeSync(err); closeSync(diagnostic); }
}
